<div align="left"><table class="RegBitDef"><col width="114.37px"/><col width="499.80px"/><tr height="22.67px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bits 31:6</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL">Reserved, must be kept at reset value.</p></td></tr><tr height="54.67px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 5</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">ITSMF</span>: Internal timestamp masked flag</p><p class="TableIndent">This flag is set by hardware when a timestamp on the internal event occurs and timestampinterrupt is raised.</p></td></tr><tr height="85.33px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 4</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">TSOVMF</span>: Timestamp overflow masked flag</p><p class="TableIndent">This flag is set by hardware when a timestamp interrupt occurs while TSMF is already set.</p><p class="TableIndent">It is recommended to check and then clear TSOVF only after clearing the TSF bit. Otherwise, an overflow might not be noticed if a timestamp event occurs immediately before the TSF bit is cleared.</p></td></tr><tr height="56.00px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 3</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">TSMF</span>: Timestamp masked flag</p><p class="TableIndent">This flag is set by hardware when a timestamp interrupt occurs.</p><p class="TableIndent">If ITSF flag is set, TSF must be cleared together with ITSF.</p></td></tr><tr height="70.67px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 2</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">WUTMF</span>: Wakeup timer masked flag</p><p class="TableIndent">This flag is set by hardware when the wakeup timer interrupt occurs.</p><p class="TableIndent">This flag must be cleared by software at least 1.5 RTCCLK periods before WUTF is set to 1 again.</p></td></tr><tr height="40.00px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 1</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">ALRBMF</span>: Alarm B masked flag</p><p class="TableBodyL">This flag is set by hardware when the alarm B interrupt occurs.</p></td></tr><tr height="40.00px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 0</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">ALRAMF</span>: Alarm A masked flag</p><p class="TableIndent">This flag is set by hardware when the alarm A interrupt occurs.</p></td></tr></table></div>


**Note:**

The bits of this register are cleared few APB clock cycles after setting their corresponding clear bit in the RTC\_SCR register. After clearing the flag, read it until it is read at 0 before leaving the interrupt routine.

### 37.6.20 RTC status clear register (RTC\_SCR)

Address offset: 0x5C

Backup domain reset value: 0x0000 0000

System reset: not affected

<div align="left"><table class="RegBitDef"><col width="114.37px"/><col width="499.80px"/><tr data-conditional="nonuser" height="40.00px" style="display: none"><td data-conditional="nonuser" style="display: none"><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 15</p></td><td data-conditional="nonuser" style="display: none"><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">CIAF</span>: Clear illegal access flag</p><p class="TableIndent">Writing 1 in this bit clears the IAF bit in the RTC_SR register.</p></td></tr><tr height="22.67px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bits 31:6</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL">Reserved, must be kept at reset value.</p></td></tr><tr height="40.00px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 5</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">CITSF</span>: Clear internal timestamp flag</p><p class="TableIndent">Writing 1 in this bit clears the ITSF bit in the RTC_SR register.</p></td></tr><tr height="85.33px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 4</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">CTSOVF</span>: Clear timestamp overflow flag</p><p class="TableIndent">Writing 1 in this bit clears the TSOVF bit in the RTC_SR register.</p><p class="TableIndent">It is recommended to check and then clear TSOVF only after clearing the TSF bit. Otherwise, an overflow might not be noticed if a timestamp event occurs immediately before the TSF bit is cleared.</p></td></tr><tr height="56.00px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 3</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">CTSF</span>: Clear timestamp flag</p><p class="TableIndent">Writing 1 in this bit clears the TSOVF bit in the RTC_SR register.</p><p class="TableIndent">If ITSF flag is set, TSF must be cleared together with ITSF by setting CRSF and CITSF.</p></td></tr><tr height="40.00px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 2</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">CWUTF</span>: Clear wakeup timer flag</p><p class="TableIndent">Writing 1 in this bit clears the WUTF bit in the RTC_SR register.</p></td></tr><tr height="40.00px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 1</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">CALRBF</span>: Clear alarm B flag</p><p class="TableIndent">Writing 1 in this bit clears the ALRBF bit in the RTC_SR register.</p></td></tr><tr height="40.00px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 0</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">CALRAF</span>: Clear alarm A flag</p><p class="TableIndent">Writing 1 in this bit clears the ALRAF bit in the RTC_SR register.</p></td></tr></table></div>
